    public abstract class ContactCenterRetentionPolicyBase<TModel, TIndex> : CrestApps.OrchardCore.ContactCenter.Core.Services.Retention.IContactCenterRetentionPolicy
        where TModel : CrestApps.Core.Models.CatalogItem
        where TIndex : CrestApps.Core.Data.YesSql.Indexes.CatalogItemIndex
    {
        protected ContactCenterRetentionPolicyBase(YesSql.ISession session, CrestApps.Core.Services.ICatalog<TModel> catalog) { }
        public abstract string EntityName { get; }
        public System.Type IndexType { get; }
        protected virtual bool IsSubjectToLegalHold { get; }
        protected virtual bool IsSubjectToReplayHorizon { get; }
        public System.Type ModelType { get; }
        protected abstract System.Linq.Expressions.Expression<System.Func<TIndex, bool>> BuildExpiredPredicate(System.DateTime cutoffUtc);
        protected virtual double GetEntityFloorDays(CrestApps.OrchardCore.ContactCenter.Core.Models.ContactCenterRetentionOptions options) { }
        public System.Linq.Expressions.LambdaExpression GetExpiredPredicate(System.DateTime cutoffUtc) { }
        protected abstract double GetRetentionDays(CrestApps.OrchardCore.ContactCenter.Core.Models.ContactCenterRetentionOptions options);
        public System.Threading.Tasks.Task<int> PurgeBatchAsync(System.DateTime cutoffUtc, int batchSize, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken = default) { }
        public bool TryGetCutoff(System.DateTime nowUtc, CrestApps.OrchardCore.ContactCenter.Core.Models.ContactCenterRetentionOptions options, out System.DateTime cutoffUtc) { }
        protected virtual System.Threading.Tasks.Task<bool> TryPrepareForDeletionAsync(TModel record, System.Threading.CancellationToken cancellationToken) { }
    }
